) to derive

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\frac{\alpha}{4}|\nabla^{2}Q|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}|v|^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{8}\frac{|\nabla(Q-\pi(Q)|^{2}}{L}\right)(x,s)\phi^{2}\,dx+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v|^{2}\phi^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\nabla^{2}Q_{0}|^{2}+|v_{0}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{0}-\pi(Q_{0}))|^{2}}{L}\right)\phi^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\frac{|Q_{L,0}-\pi(Q_{L,0})|^{2}}{L}|\nabla Q_{L,0}|^{2}+\frac{|Q-\pi(Q)|^{2}}{L}|\nabla Q|^{2}(x,s)\right)\phi^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}2\eta\Big(|\nabla^{3}Q|^{2}+|\nabla\partial_{t}Q|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla^{2}(Q-\pi(Q))|^{2}}{L}\Big)\phi^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(|\nabla^{2}Q|^{2}+|\nabla v|^{2}+|\partial_{t}Q|^{2})(e(Q,v)\phi^{2}+|\nabla\phi|^{2})\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\frac{|\nabla(Q-\pi(Q))|^{2}}{L}+e^{2}(Q,v)\right)(e(Q,v)\phi^{2}+|\nabla\phi|^{2})\,dxdt.

(2.67)

Substituting (
2.62
) and choosing suitable
\eta
, we obtain the estimate for
\nabla v
. Combining the resulting expression with (
2.62
), we proved (
2.49
).
∎

3.
Proof of Theorem
2

In this section, we will prove Theorem
2
. At first,
we derive a local estimate on the pressure
P_{L}(x,t)
.

Lemma 3.1
.

Let
(Q_{L},v_{L})
be a strong solution to (
1.11
)-(
1.13
) in
\mathbb{R}^{3}\times(T_{0},T_{L})
. Assume that
Q\in S_{\delta}
with sufficiently small
\delta
on
\mathbb{R}^{3}\times(0,T_{L})
and

\sup_{T_{0}\leq t\leq T_{L},x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}(x,t)|^{3}+|v_{L}(x,t)|^{3}dx\leq\varepsilon_{0}^{3}.

(3.1)

Then for any
t\in(T_{0},T_{L})
, there exists a constant
c_{L}(t)\in\mathbb{R}
such that the pressure
P_{L}
satisfies the following estimate

\displaystyle\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}|P_{L}-c_{L}^{\ast}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2})+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R^{2}}(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2})\,dxdt.

(3.2)

Proof.

The proof is essentially the same as the one in
[
11
]
. For completeness, we outline an approach here. Let
\phi
be a cut-off function satisfying

0\leq\phi\leq 1
,
\text{supp }\phi\subset B_{2R}(x_{0})
for some
x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
and
|\nabla\phi|\leq\frac{C}{R}
. Note that the pressure
P_{L}
satisfies

-\Delta P_{L}=\nabla_{ij}^{2}\left([Q_{L},H(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})]_{ij}-\sigma_{ij}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})+v^{i}_{L}v^{j}_{L}\right)\quad\text{on}~~\mathbb{R}^{3}\times[T_{0},T_{L}],

which implies
P_{L}=\mathcal{R}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{j}(F^{ij}),
and

|F^{ij}|=|[Q_{L},H(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})]_{ij}-\sigma_{ij}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})+v^{i}_{L}v^{j}_{L}|\leq C(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|+|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}),

where
\mathcal{R}_{i}
is the
i
-th Riesz transform on
\mathbb{R}^{3}
. Then we have

(P_{L}-c_{L}^{\ast})\phi=\mathcal{R}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{j}(F^{ij}\phi)+[\phi,\mathcal{R}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{j}](F^{ij})-c_{L}^{\ast}\phi

(3.3)

for a cut-off function
\phi
, where the commutator
[\phi,\mathcal{R}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{j}]
is defined by

[\phi,\mathcal{R}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{j}](\cdot)=\phi\mathcal{R}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{j}(\cdot)-\mathcal{R}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{j}(\cdot\,\phi).

By using the Riesz operator maps
L^{q}
into
L^{q}

spaces for any
1<q<+\infty
and the assumption (
3.1
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\mathcal{R}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{j}(F^{ij}\phi)|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R(x_{0})}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}dx\,dt+\frac{C}{R^{2}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R(x_{0})}}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt.

(3.4)

Since
\text{supp}\,\phi\subset B_{2R(x_{0})}
, the commutator can be expressed as

\displaystyle[\phi,\mathcal{R}_{i}\mathcal{R}_{j}](F^{ij})(x,t)-c_{L}^{\ast}(t)\phi(x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{B_{4R}(x_{0})}\frac{(\phi(x)-\phi(y))(x_{i}-y_{i})(x_{j}-y_{j})}{|x-y|^{5}}F^{ij}(y,t)\,dy

\displaystyle\quad+\phi(x)\left[\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\backslash B_{4R}(x_{0})}\frac{(x_{i}-y_{i})(x_{j}-y_{j})}{|x-y|^{5}}F^{ij}(y,t)\,dy-c_{L}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle:f_{1}(x,t)+f_{2}(x,t).

(3.5)

By using the Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev inequality (c.f.
[
16
]
)

\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{f(y)}{|x-y|^{n-\alpha}}\,dy\right\|_{L^{q}(\mathbb{R}^{n})}\leq C\|f\|_{L^{r}(\mathbb{R}^{n})},\quad\frac{1}{q}=\frac{1}{r}-\frac{\alpha}{n}

and the Hölder inequality, a standard covering argument yields

\displaystyle\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f_{1}(x,s)|^{2}\,dxdt\leq CR^{-2}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\|(F^{ij})\chi_{B_{4R(x_{0})}}\|_{L^{\frac{6}{5}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{R^{2}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\|(|\nabla Q_{L}|+|v_{L}|)\chi_{B_{4R}(x_{0})}\|_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\|(|\nabla Q_{L}|+|v_{L}|)\chi_{B_{4R}(x_{0})}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{R^{2}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\|\chi_{B_{4R}(x_{0})}\|^{2}_{L^{3}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\|(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|)\chi_{B_{4R}(x_{0})}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R^{2}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{4R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{4R}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt,

(3.6)

where
\chi_{B_{4R}(x_{0})}(x)=1
for
x\in B_{4R}(x_{0})
and
0
for
x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\backslash B_{4R}(x_{0})
. Choosing

c_{L}^{\ast}(t)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\backslash B_{4R}(x_{0})}\frac{(x_{0i}-y_{i})(x_{0j}-y_{j})}{|x_{0}-y|^{5}}F^{ij}(y,t)\,dy

and using the Hölder inequality, we estimate

\displaystyle\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f_{2}(z,s)|^{2}\,dzdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CR^{5}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\left|\sum^{\infty}_{k=4}\frac{C}{(kR)^{4}}\int_{B_{(k+1)R(x_{0})}\backslash B_{kR(x_{0})}}F^{ij}(x,t)\,dx\right|^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\sum^{\infty}_{k=4}k^{-4}\int_{B_{R(y)}}|F^{ij}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R^{2}}(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2})+(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2})\,dxdt.

(3.7)

Combining (
3.4
), (
3.6
) with (
3.7
), we can apply a standard covering argument to complete the proof.
∎

Using Lemma
2.5
and Lemma
2.6
, we have:

Lemma 3.2
.

Let
(Q_{L},v_{L})
be a strong solution of
\eqref{RBE1}-\eqref{RBE3}
in
\mathbb{R}^{3}\times[T_{0},T_{L})
with initial value
(Q_{L,T_{0}},v_{L,T_{0}})\in H^{2}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3};S_{0})\times H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3};\mathbb{R}^{3})
and
\div v=0
. Assume that
Q\in S_{\delta}
for sufficiently small
\delta
on
\mathbb{R}^{3}\times(0,T_{L})
. There exist two constants
\varepsilon_{0}
and
R
that

\displaystyle\sup_{T_{0}\leq t\leq T_{L},x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{3}+|v_{L}|^{3}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{3}}{L^{\frac{3}{2}}}\,dx\leq\varepsilon_{0}^{3}.

(3.8)

Then we have

\displaystyle\sup_{T_{0}\leq s\leq T_{L},x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{R}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}\left(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L}\right)(x,s)\,dx

\displaystyle+\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{R}\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+|\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{R}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|v_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}})|^{2}}{L}\,dx+C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\frac{(T_{L}-T_{0})}{R^{2}}

(3.9)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{T_{0}\leq s\leq T_{L},x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}R\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}\left(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\right)(x,s)\,dx

\displaystyle+\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}R\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{3}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}v_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla^{2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CR\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{R}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|v_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}})|^{2}}{L}\,dx+C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\frac{(T_{L}-T_{0})}{R^{2}}.

(3.10)

Proof.

Let
\{B_{R}(x_{i})\}^{\infty}_{i=1}
be a standard open cover of
\mathbb{R}^{3}
such that at each
x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, there are finite intersections of open balls
B_{R}(x_{i})
.
Let
\phi\in C_{0}^{\infty}(B_{2R}(x_{0}))
with
\phi\equiv 1
on
B_{R}(x_{0})
,
|\nabla\phi|\leq\frac{C}{R}
and
|\nabla^{2}\phi|\leq\frac{C}{R^{2}}
. Recall from Lemma
2.5
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{R}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}\left(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L}\right)(x,s)\,dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{R}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{R}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|v_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}})|^{2}}{L}\,dx

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{R}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}\left(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta}{R}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}|P_{L}-c_{L}^{*}(t)|^{2}\,dxdt+\frac{C(\eta)}{R^{3}}\int_{T_{0}}^{s}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

(3.11)

for some small
\eta
to be chosen later. Using Hölder’s inequality and (
3.8
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{T_{0}\leq s\leq T_{L},x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{R}\int^{s}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}\left(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L}\right)(x,s)\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}(T_{L}-T_{0}).

(3.12)

Then, using the Sobolev inequality, (
3.8
) and (
3.12
), we find

\displaystyle\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{R}\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C}{R}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\sum_{i}\left(\int_{B_{R}(x_{i})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{3}\,dx\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}\left(\int_{B_{R}(x_{i})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{6}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}\,dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R}\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt+\frac{C}{R}\sup_{T_{0}\leq s\leq T_{L},y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R}\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt+C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\frac{T_{L}-T_{0}}{R^{2}}.

(3.13)

Similarly, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{R}\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}\left(|v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R}\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt+C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\frac{T_{L}-T_{0}}{R^{2}}.

(3.14)

Substituting (
3.12
)-(
3.14
) into (
3.11
), using Lemma
3.1
and taking the supremum of
x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we prove (
3.9
) by choosing
\eta
sufficiently small.

To show (
3.10
), recall from Lemma
2.6
that

\displaystyle R\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}\left(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\right)(x,T_{L})\,dx

\displaystyle+R\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{3}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla^{2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CR\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}}))|^{2}}{L}\,dx

\displaystyle+CR\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}\frac{|Q_{L,0}-\pi(Q_{L,0})|^{2}}{L}|\nabla Q_{L,0}|^{2}+\left(\frac{|Q-\pi(Q)|^{2}}{L}|\nabla Q|^{2}\right)(x,T_{L})\,dx

\displaystyle+CR\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}e(Q_{L},v_{L})\left(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+|\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle+CR\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}e(Q_{L},v_{L})\left(\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}+e^{2}(Q_{L},v_{L})\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{R}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+|\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q-\pi(Q))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{R}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}e^{2}(Q_{L},v_{L})+|P_{L}-c_{L}^{*}(t)|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CR\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}}))|^{2}}{L}\,dx

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}R\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}(x,T_{L})|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{R}\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}|\nabla Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|v_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}})|^{2}}{L}\,dx

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}R\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}v_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla^{2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\frac{C(T_{L}-T_{0})}{R^{2}}+CR\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}e^{3}(Q_{L},v_{L})\,dxdt.

(3.15)

Here we used the argument in (
3.13
), Lemma
3.1
and substituted (
3.9
). Applying the argument in (
3.13
) twice, we deduce the last term in (
3.15
) to

\displaystyle R\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}e^{3}(Q_{L},v_{L})\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CR\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{L}}\int_{B_{2R}(x_{0})}e(Q_{L},v_{L})\left(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{4}+|v_{L}|^{4}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{4}}{L^{2}}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle\ \leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}R\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}|\nabla|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}|^{2}+|\nabla|v_{L}|^{2}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}|^{2}}{L^{2}}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R}\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{4}+|v_{L}|^{4}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{4}}{L^{2}}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}R\sup_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int^{T_{L}}_{T_{0}}\int_{B_{R}(y)}|\nabla^{3}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla^{2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{R}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|v_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}})|^{2}}{L}\,dx+\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\frac{C(T_{L}-T_{0})}{R^{2}}.

(3.16)

Here we also used (
3.9
). Substituting (
3.16
) into (
3.15
) and then taking the supremum of
x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
on the resulting expression, we obtain (
3.10
).
∎

Using the Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation, we establish a uniform local existence of the strong solutions:

Proposition 3.1
.

Assume that
(Q_{L,T_{0}},v_{L,T_{0}})
satisfies

\displaystyle\|Q_{L,T_{0}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v_{L,T_{0}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\frac{\|Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}})\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}}{L}\leq M

(3.17)

for some
M>0
. Then there are uniform constants
T_{M},R_{0}
in
L
such that the system (
1.11
)-(
1.13
) with initial data
(Q_{L,T_{0}},v_{L,T_{0}})
has
a unique strong solution
(Q_{L},v_{L})
in
\mathbb{R}^{3}\times[T_{0},T_{M}]
satisfying

\displaystyle\sup_{T_{0}\leq t\leq T_{M},x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R_{0}}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{3}+|v_{L}|^{3}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{3}}{L^{\frac{3}{2}}}\,dx\leq\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{3}}{2}

(3.18)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{T_{0}\leq s\leq T_{M}}\left(\|\nabla Q_{L}(s)\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|v_{L}(s)\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{1}{L}\|Q_{L}(s)-\pi(Q_{L}(s))\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(T_{0},T_{M};H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(T_{0},T_{M};H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla v_{L}\|_{L^{2}(T_{0},T_{M};H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}+\frac{1}{L}\|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leq C\left(1+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R_{0}^{2}}\right)M.

(3.19)

Proof.

It follows from the Sobolev embedding theorem that for any
0<\varepsilon_{0}<1
, there exists a positive constant

R_{0}=:\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{C_{s}^{2}N^{2}M}
(c.f.
[
11
]
) such that

\displaystyle\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R_{0}}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{3}+|v_{L,T_{0}}|^{3}+\frac{|Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}})|^{3}}{L^{\frac{3}{2}}}\,dx\leq\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{3}}{N^{3}},

(3.20)

where
N>1
is an absolute constant independent of
L
and
M
to be chosen later. By using the Gagliardo–Nirenberg interpolation (c.f.
[
11
]
) at
T_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\dist(Q_{L}(T_{0});S_{*})=\|\dist(Q_{L}(T_{0});S_{*})\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|Q_{L}(T_{0})-\pi(Q_{L}(T_{0}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}(Q_{L}(T_{0})-\pi(Q_{L}(T_{0})))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(LM)^{\frac{1}{8}}\Big(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}(T_{0})|^{2}+|\partial_{Q}\pi|^{2}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}(T_{0})|^{2}+|\partial^{2}_{QQ}\pi|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}(T_{0})|^{2}\,dx\Big)^{\frac{3}{8}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{d}L^{\frac{1}{8}}M^{\frac{1}{4}}\leq\frac{\delta}{2},

where we have used the condition (
3.17
) and chosen
L\leq\left(\frac{\delta}{2C_{d}M^{\frac{1}{4}}}\right)^{8}
.

Using Theorem
1
, there is a unique local strong solution
(Q_{L},v_{L})
such that
(Q_{L},v_{L})
is continuous in
t
, which follows from the Sobolev inequality (c.f.
[
15
]
). Then there is a time
T_{L}^{*}\in(T_{0},T_{L}]
such that

\displaystyle\dist(Q_{L};S_{*})\leq\delta\text{ on }\mathbb{R}^{3}\times(T_{0},T_{L}^{*}).

(3.21)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{T_{0}\leq t\leq T_{L}^{*},x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R_{0}}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{3}+|v_{L}|^{3}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{3}}{L^{\frac{3}{2}}}\,dx\leq{\varepsilon_{0}^{3}}.

(3.22)

Using (
3.17
), (
3.20
),
R_{0}=\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{C_{s}^{2}N^{2}M}
and choosing
(T_{L}^{*}-T_{0})\leq\sigma R_{0}^{2}
for some small
\sigma
to be chosen later, we have

\displaystyle CR_{0}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R_{0}}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}}))|^{2}}{L}\,dx

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{R_{0}}\sup_{x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+|v_{L,T_{0}}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L,T_{0}}-\pi(Q_{L,T_{0}})|^{2}}{L}\,dx+\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\frac{C(T_{L}-T_{0})}{R_{0}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CMR_{0}+\frac{C|B_{R_{0}}|^{\frac{1}{3}}}{R_{0}}\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{N^{2}}+C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\sigma\leq\frac{C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{N^{2}}+C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\sigma.

(3.23)

By using the Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation and applying (
3.23
) to (
3.9
)-(
3.10
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{T_{0}\leq t\leq T^{*}_{L},x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R_{0}}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{3}+|v_{L}|^{3}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{3}}{L^{\frac{3}{2}}}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\sup_{T_{0}\leq t\leq T^{*}_{L},x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{R_{0}}\int_{B_{R_{0}}(x_{0})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L}\,dx\right)^{3/2}

\displaystyle+C\sup_{T_{0}\leq t\leq T^{*}_{L},x_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(R_{0}\int_{B_{R_{0}}(x_{0})}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dx\right)^{3/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{C_{1}\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{N^{2}}+C_{2}\sigma\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\right)^{3/2}\leq\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{3}}{2},

where we choose
N\geq 2(C_{1}+1)^{\frac{1}{2}}
and
\sigma\leq\min\{\frac{2}{C_{2}},1\}
. Thus we prove (
3.22
) up to the uniform time
T_{M}=T_{0}+\sigma R_{0}^{2}
.

For (
3.19
), using a standard open cover
\{B_{R}(x_{i})\}_{i=1}^{\infty}
for
\mathbb{R}^{3}
with finite intersections at each
x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
, the Hölder inequality and the Sobolev inequality, we find

\displaystyle\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{4}\,dxdt\leq\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{M}}\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}\left(\int_{B_{R_{0}}(x_{i})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{3}\,dx\right)^{\frac{2}{3}}\left(\int_{B_{R_{0}}(x_{i})}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{6}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{3}}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt+C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\frac{T_{M}-T_{0}}{R^{2}}\sup_{T_{0}\leq s\leq T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{L}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt+\frac{1}{2}\sup_{T_{0}\leq s\leq T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{L}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx.

(3.24)

Similarly, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}\left(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sup_{T_{0}\leq s\leq T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L}\right)(x,s)\,dx.

(3.25)

Choosing
\phi\equiv 1
in Lemma
2.5
, using (
3.17
) and (
3.25
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{T_{0}\leq s\leq T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L}\right)(x,s)\,dx

\displaystyle+\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+|\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt\leq CM.

(3.26)

Using the method in (
3.25
) and substituting (
3.26
) to Lemma
2.6
with
\phi\equiv 1
, we find

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\right)(x,s)\,dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}v_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla^{2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CM+C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}(x,T_{M})|^{2}\,dx+\frac{C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R_{0}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{L}(x,T_{M})|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{L,0}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\frac{C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R_{0}^{2}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+|\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+\frac{|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{C\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R_{0}^{2}}\int_{T_{0}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{4}+|v_{L}|^{4}+\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{4}}{L^{2}}\,dxdt\leq C\left(1+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R_{0}^{2}}\right)M.

(3.27)

Combining (
3.26
) with (
3.27
), we prove (
3.19
).
∎

Proof of Theorem
2
.

By using Proposition
3.1
and Lemma
3.2
, there exist two uniform positive constants
T_{1}

and
L_{\ast}
such that for any
L\leq L_{\ast}
, the strong solution
(Q_{L},v_{L})
to (
1.11
)-(
1.13
) satisfies

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{1}}\left(\|\nabla Q_{L}(t)\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|v_{L}(t)\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{1}{L}\|Q_{L}(t)-\pi(Q_{L}(t))\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(0,T_{1};H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(0,T_{1};H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla v_{L}\|_{L^{2}(0,T_{1};H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}+\frac{1}{L}\|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})\|_{H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leq C\left(1+\frac{\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}{R^{2}}\right)M.

(3.28)

Since the pressure
P_{L}
satisfies
(
3.3
), using (
3.28
), we find

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T_{1}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|P_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt\leq\int_{0}^{T_{1}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{4}+|v_{L}|^{4}+|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt\leq C

(3.29)

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T_{1}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla P_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{T_{1}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\nabla[Q_{L},\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})]|^{2}+|\nabla\sigma(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{L}\otimes v_{L})|^{2}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{T_{1}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{L}|^{6}+|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}|v_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt\leq C.

(3.30)

Multiplying (
1.11
) with
(Q_{L}-Q_{e})
, one can show that
(Q_{L}-Q_{e})\in L^{\infty}(0,T_{1};L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))
.
Then, letting
L\to 0
(up to a subsequence) and utilizing the Aubin-Lions Lemma, we have

\displaystyle Q_{L}\rightharpoonup

\displaystyle Q\text{ in }L^{2}(0,T_{1};H^{3}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))\cap H^{1}(0,T_{1};H^{2}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}Q_{L}\rightharpoonup

\displaystyle\partial_{t}Q\text{ in }L^{2}(0,T_{1};H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})),

\displaystyle v_{L}\rightharpoonup

\displaystyle v\text{ in }L^{2}(0,T_{1};H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))\cap H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}\times(0,T_{1})),

\displaystyle\partial_{t}v_{L}\rightharpoonup

\displaystyle\partial_{t}v\text{ in }L^{2}(0,T_{1};L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})),

\displaystyle P_{L}\rightharpoonup

\displaystyle P\text{ in }L^{2}(0,T_{1};H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})),

\displaystyle(\nabla Q_{L},v_{L})\rightarrow

\displaystyle(Q,v)\text{ in }L^{2}(0,T_{1};H^{1}(B_{R}(0)))\cap C([0,T_{1}];L^{2}(B_{R}(0)))

for any
R\in(0,\infty)
. We claim

\displaystyle\lim_{L\to 0}\frac{1}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f_{B}(Q_{L}(x,s))\,dx=0

(3.31)

for all
s\in[0,T_{1}]
. To prove this claim, we need to estimate the
L^{2}
-norm of
\partial_{t}Q_{L}
. We differentiate (
2.36
) in
t
, multiply by

R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}}
. To estimate the right-hand side term on
g_{B}(Q_{L})
, we apply Lemma
2.1
with
\xi=\partial_{t}Q_{L}
and Lemma
2.2
to obtain

\displaystyle\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\partial_{t}\frac{g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})}{L},R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{L}\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}_{ij}\tilde{Q}_{kl}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})\partial_{t}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{ij}\partial_{t}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{kl}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|\partial_{t}\tilde{Q}_{L}|}{L^{\frac{1}{2}}}|\partial_{t}R_{Q_{L}}\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|}{L^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\lambda}{4}\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|\partial_{t}\tilde{Q}_{L}|^{2}}{L}\,dxdt+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L}|\partial_{t}Q|^{2}\,dx\leq CM.

(3.32)

Integrating by part and using (
3.18
), (
3.28
) and Young’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\partial_{t}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})R_{Q_{L}}),\,R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\partial_{t}\nabla_{k}[\partial_{p_{ij}^{k}}f_{E}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})-\partial_{Q_{ij}}f_{E}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})],\partial_{t}Q_{L}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\partial_{t}R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\nabla_{k}[\partial_{p_{ij}^{k}}f_{E}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})-\partial_{Q_{ij}}f_{E}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})],\,\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla_{k}[\partial_{p_{ij}^{k}}f_{E}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})-\partial_{Q_{ij}}f_{E}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})]\partial_{t}R_{Q_{L}},\,R^{T}{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt\leq CM.

(3.33)

Similarly, for the left-hand side, we find

\displaystyle\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\partial_{t}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\big(\partial_{t}Q_{L}+(v\cdot\nabla Q_{L})+[Q_{L},\Omega_{L}]\big)R_{Q_{L}}),R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}|R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}\,dxdt-C\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\partial_{t}v_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle-C\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}\left(|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}Q_{L}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}Q_{0}|^{2}\,dx-CM.

(3.34)

Here we used
\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}v_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt\leq CM
due to (
1.11
).

In view of (
3.32
)-(
3.34
), we derive

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq T_{1}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}Q_{L}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}Q_{L}(x,0)|^{2}\,dx+CM\leq CM,

(3.35)

where we used (
1.13
), (
3.18
), (
3.28
), (
3.30
) and
g_{B}(Q_{0})=0
for
Q_{0}\in S_{*}
.

Using (
1.13
), (
3.35
) and (
3.28
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})|^{2}}{L^{2}}\,dx=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|g_{B}(Q_{L})|^{2}}{L^{2}}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}+|v_{L}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla v_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{L}|^{4}\,dx\leq CM.

(3.36)

For any
Q_{L}\in S_{\delta}
with a sufficiently small
\delta>0
, we take the Taylor expansion and adapt the argument in (
2.17
) with Corollary
1
to obtain

\displaystyle 0=f_{B}(Q^{+})=

\displaystyle f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})+\nabla_{\tilde{Q}_{ij}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})(\tilde{Q}_{L}-Q^{+})_{ij}

\displaystyle+\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}_{ij}\tilde{Q}_{kl}}f_{B}(Q_{\tau})(\tilde{Q}_{L}-Q^{+})_{ij}(\tilde{Q}_{L}-Q^{+})_{kl}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})-g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{ij}(\tilde{Q}_{L}-Q^{+})_{ij},

(3.37)

where
Q_{\tau}
is an intermediate point between
\tilde{Q}_{L}
and
Q^{+}
. Then using (
3.37
) and (
3.36
), we find

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f_{B}(Q_{L})\,dx=\frac{1}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})\,dx\leq\frac{1}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L}),Q^{+}-\tilde{Q}_{L}\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})|^{2}}{L^{2}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\pi(Q_{L})-Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Using (
3.28
) that
\|Q_{L}(s)-\pi(Q_{L}(s))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leq CL
for
s\in[0,T_{1})
, we have

\lim_{L\to 0}\|Q_{L}(s)-\pi(Q_{L}(s))\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T_{1};L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}=0.

Then, we prove the claim (
3.31
).

For
\varphi\in C^{\infty}_{0}(\mathbb{R}^{3},S_{0})
, we define

\displaystyle\Phi_{ij}(Q,\varphi)=

\displaystyle s^{-1}_{+}(Q_{jl}+\frac{s_{+}}{3}\delta_{jl})\varphi_{il}+s^{-1}_{+}(Q_{il}+\frac{s_{+}}{3}\delta_{il})\varphi_{jl}

\displaystyle-2s_{+}^{-2}(Q_{ij}+\frac{s_{+}}{3}\delta_{ij})(Q_{lm}+\frac{s_{+}}{3}\delta_{lm})\varphi_{lm}.

As
L\to 0
, we multiply (
1.13
) by
\Phi_{ij}(Q,\varphi)
and obtain

\displaystyle\left\langle(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla)Q+[Q,\Omega],\Phi(Q,\varphi)\right\rangle=(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla)(s_{+}u_{i}u_{j})(2u_{j}u_{l}\varphi_{il})

\displaystyle+\Big(s_{+}(u_{i}u_{k}-\frac{1}{3}\delta_{ik})\Omega_{kj}-s_{+}(u_{k}u_{j}-\frac{1}{3}\delta_{kj})\Omega_{ik}\Big)\Phi_{ij}(Q,\varphi)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle(\partial_{t}+v\cdot\nabla)Q+[Q,\Omega],\varphi\right\rangle,

(3.38)

where we used that
|Q|=\sqrt{\frac{3}{2}}s_{+}
and
|u|=1
. Multiplying (
1.13
) with
\Phi_{ij}(Q,\varphi)

and using the condition (
3.31
), we can apply Theorem 2 in
[
10
]
that the solution
(Q_{L},v_{L})
of (
1.11
)-(
1.13
) converges
to a solution
(Q,v)
of (
1.17
)-(
1.19
) as
L\to 0
. Taking the difference between two solutions under
L^{2}
estimates, it can be shown (c.f.
[
15
]
or
[
11
]
) that the strong solution
(Q,v)
is unique. The proof on uniqueness is similar to the claim 2 in the appendix, so we omit the details here. Let
T_{1}
be any time with
T_{1}\leq T^{*}
. Under the criteria

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{1},x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{B_{R_{0}}(x_{0})}(|\nabla Q|^{3}+|v|^{3})\,dx\leq{\varepsilon_{0}^{3}},

we can use the same methods in
[
15
]
to extend the solution passing
T_{1}
up to the maximal time
T^{*}
.
∎

4.
Smooth convergence

In this section, we will prove Theorem
3
. At first,
we obtain the following higher order estimate:

Lemma 4.1
.

Let
(Q_{L},v_{L})
be a strong solution of
\eqref{RBE1}-\eqref{RBE3}
in
\mathbb{R}^{3}\times[T_{0},T_{M})
with initial value
(Q_{L,T_{0}},v_{L,T_{0}})\in H^{2}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\times H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
and
\div v=0
. For any
\tau>T_{0}
,
s\in(\tau,T_{M}]
and any integer
m\geq 0
, there exists a positive constant
C_{m}
independently of
Q_{L}
and
L
(but depending on
m
) such that

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\leq s\leq T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\nabla^{m+1}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{m}v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{1}{L}|\nabla^{m}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}\right)(x,t)\,dx

\displaystyle+\int_{\tau}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{m+2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{m+1}v_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{m}\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{\tau}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{L}|\nabla^{m+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}\,dxdt\leq C_{m}.

(4.1)

Proof.

We prove this lemma by induction. In view of (
3.26
) and (
3.27
), one have shown (
4.1
) holds for
m=0,1
. Assume that (
4.1
) holds for
m=1,\cdots,k
with
k\geq 1
. Then we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\frac{\tau}{2}\leq s\leq T_{M}}\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\nabla^{i+1}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{i}v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{1}{L}|\nabla^{i}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}\right)(x,s)\,dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{\frac{\tau}{2}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{i+2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{i+1}v_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{i}\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{k}\int_{\frac{\tau}{2}}^{T_{M}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{L}|\nabla^{i+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}\,dxdt\leq C_{k}(\tau).

(4.2)

For
m=k
, it follows from using (
4.2
) and the mean value theorem that there exists a
\tau_{L}\in(\tau/2,\tau)
such that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{k+1}v_{L}|^{2}+\frac{1}{L}|\nabla^{k}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}\right)(x,\tau_{L})\,dx\leq C_{k}(\tau).

(4.3)

Applying the Sobolev inequality to (
4.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau_{L}\leq s\leq T_{M}}\sum_{i=0}^{k-1}\|\nabla^{i}(Q_{L}-Q_{e})(s)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leq C_{k}(\tau).

(4.4)

For functions
f_{1},f_{2}\in H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, by using a similar argument to one in (
3.24
), we observe

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f_{1}|^{2}|f_{2}|^{2}\,dx\leq C\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\|\nabla f_{2}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})},

(4.5)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f_{1}|^{2}|f_{2}|^{4}\,dx\leq C\|\nabla f_{1}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\|\nabla f_{2}\|^{4}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

(4.6)

Next, we show that (
4.1
) holds for
m=k+1
.

In order to derive the
L^{2}
-norm of
\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}
, we apply
\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}
to (
2.36
) and multiply by
\nabla^{k+2}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\nabla_{\beta}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})
to obtain

\displaystyle J_{1}:=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}\Big(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\big(\partial_{t}Q_{L}+v_{L}\cdot\nabla Q_{L}+[Q_{L},\Omega_{L}]\big)R_{Q_{L}}\Big),\nabla^{k+2}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\nabla_{\beta}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}\left(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})R_{Q_{L}}\right),\nabla^{k+2}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\nabla_{\beta}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L}),\nabla^{k+2}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\nabla_{\beta}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})\right\rangle\,dx=:J_{2}+J_{3}.

(4.7)

For the term
J_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle J_{2}\geq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}\nabla_{\nu}\left(\partial^{2}_{p^{\nu}}f_{E}\right),\nabla^{k+2}\nabla_{\beta}Q_{L}\right\rangle\,dx-\frac{\alpha}{8}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle-C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+1}\partial_{Q}f_{E}|^{2}\,dx-\eta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+2}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\nabla Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle-C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{3}}R_{Q_{L}})|^{2}\,dx

(4.8)

for some small
\eta
to be chosen later. We deduce the first term on the right-hand side in (
4.8
) from (
2.2
) that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}\left(\nabla_{\nu}\partial_{p^{\nu}_{ij}}f_{E}\right)\nabla^{k+2}\nabla_{\beta}(Q_{L})_{ij}\,dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\nu}\left(\partial^{2}_{p^{\nu}_{ij}p^{\gamma}_{mn}}f_{E}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\gamma}(Q_{L})_{mn}\right)\nabla^{k+2}\nabla_{\beta}(Q_{L})_{ij}\,dx

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\nu}\left(\partial^{2}_{p^{\nu}_{ij}Q_{mn}}f_{E}\nabla_{\beta}(Q_{L})_{mn}\right)\nabla^{k+2}\nabla_{\beta}(Q_{L})_{ij}\,dx

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\nu}_{ij}p^{\gamma}_{mn}}f_{E}\nabla^{k+1}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\gamma}(Q_{L})_{mn}\nabla^{k+1}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\nu}(Q_{L})_{ij}\,dx

\displaystyle-C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}|\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\nabla\partial^{2}_{pp}f_{E}||\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|\,dx

\displaystyle-C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}|\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}=k+1}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\partial^{2}_{pQ}f_{E}||\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla Q_{L}|\,dx

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{3\alpha}{8}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx-C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\nabla\partial^{2}_{pp}f_{E}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle-C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}=k+1}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\partial^{2}_{pQ}f_{E}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx.

(4.9)

To proceed further, we need to examine the right-hand side of (
4.8
)-(
4.9
). Using (
4.2
), (
4.4
)-(
4.6
), the last term in (
4.8
) becomes

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+2}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\nabla Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}=k+2}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{3}}R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{k+1}Q_{L}|^{2}(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{L}|^{4})\,dx

\displaystyle+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k}Q_{L}|^{2}(|\nabla^{3}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{L}|^{6})\,dx

\displaystyle+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu_{1}+\cdots+\mu_{3}=k+3\\
\max\{\mu_{1},\mu_{2},\mu_{3}\}\leq k-1\end{subarray}}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{3}}R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx+C\|\nabla Q_{L}(x)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+C\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{k+1}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx\right)\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx+1\right)

\displaystyle+C\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx\right)\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx\right)^{2}\,

\displaystyle+C\|\nabla^{k}Q_{L}(x)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{k+1}Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla^{k}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+C\|\nabla^{k}Q_{L}(x)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+C(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1)^{2}.

(4.10)

Similarly, we can estimate the remaining terms in (
4.4
)-(
4.6
) as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}(\partial_{Q}f_{E})|^{2}+\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{3}}R_{Q_{L}})|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\nabla\partial^{2}_{pp}f_{E}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}=k+1}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\partial^{2}_{pQ}f_{E}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx\leq C(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1)^{2}.

(4.11)

Substituting (
4.9
)-(
4.11
) into (
4.8
) and choosing sufficiently small
\eta
, we have

\displaystyle J_{2}\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{4}\|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}-C(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1)^{2}.

(4.12)

To estimate
J_{3}
, utilizing (
4.2
), the Sobolev inequality and (
4.6
)-(
4.4
), it follows from Corollary
1
that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}_{ij}\tilde{Q}_{kl}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})\nabla^{k+2}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{ij}\nabla^{k+2}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{kl}\,dx

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\lambda}{2}|\nabla^{k+2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}\,dx-\frac{C}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{L}|^{4})\,dx

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k-1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}(|\nabla^{3}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{L}|^{6})\,dx

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hskip-8.61108pt\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu_{1}\leq k-2,\\
\max\{\mu_{2},\cdots,\mu_{k+3}\}\leq k-1\end{subarray}}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}Q_{L}|^{2}\cdots|\nabla^{\mu_{k+3}}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}(|\nabla^{k}Q_{L}|^{2}+1)\,dx

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2}(|\nabla^{k+1}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{k}Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2}+1)\,dx

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}(|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla^{k+1}Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{L}|^{2})\,dx

\displaystyle-\frac{C}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L})|^{2}|\nabla^{k}Q_{L}|^{2}(|\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{L}|^{4}+1)\,dx

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\lambda}{2L}\|\nabla^{k+2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle-C(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1)\left(\frac{1}{L}\|\nabla^{k+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1\right).

(4.13)

Repeating the argument in (
4.10
), one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\|\nabla^{k+2}\tilde{Q}_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2L}\|\nabla^{k+2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle-C(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1)\left(\frac{1}{L}\|\nabla^{k+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1\right).

(4.14)

Applying Lemma
2.2
to
J_{3}
and combining with (
4.13
)-(
4.14
) yields

\displaystyle J_{3}=

\displaystyle\frac{(-1)^{k+2}}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{2k+2}\nabla_{\beta}(g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})),\nabla_{\beta}\tilde{Q}_{L}\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle+\frac{(-1)^{k+2}}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{2k+2}\nabla_{\beta}(g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})),\nabla_{\beta}R_{Q_{L}}^{T}(\pi(Q_{L})-Q_{L})R_{Q_{L}}\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle+\frac{(-1)^{k+2}}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{2k+2}\nabla_{\beta}(g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})),R_{Q_{L}}^{T}(\pi(Q_{L})-Q_{L})\nabla_{\beta}R_{Q_{L}}\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}_{ij}\tilde{Q}_{kl}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})\nabla^{k+2}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{kl}\nabla^{k+2}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{ij}\,dx

\displaystyle-C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|\nabla^{k+2}\tilde{Q}_{L}|}{L^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\nabla\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})|\frac{|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla\tilde{Q}_{L}|}{L^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,dx

\displaystyle-C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|\nabla^{k+2}g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})|}{L^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\mu_{1}<\mu_{2}\leq\mu_{3}\leq k+2\\
\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}=k+2\end{subarray}}\frac{|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}(Q-\pi(Q_{L}))|}{L^{\frac{1}{2}}}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}R_{Q_{L}}^{T}||\nabla^{\mu_{3}}R_{Q_{L}}|\,dx

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{4L}\|\nabla^{k+2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle-C(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1)\left(\frac{1}{L}\|\nabla^{k+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1\right),

(4.15)

where we used that

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{|\nabla^{k+2}g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})|^{2}}{L}\,dx\leq C\|\nabla^{k+2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+C(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1)\left(\frac{1}{L}\|\nabla^{k+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1\right).

Applying (
4.2
), (
4.4
)-(
4.6
) to the left-hand side of (
4.7
) and then substituting (
4.10
), we obtain

\displaystyle J_{1}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{t}Q_{L},\nabla^{k+2}\nabla_{\beta}Q_{L}\right\rangle\,dx+\eta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+2}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\nabla_{\beta}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{3}}R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}=k+1}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}(v_{L}\cdot\nabla Q_{L}+[Q_{L},\Omega_{L}])|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{3}}R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{\alpha}{8}\|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+C\|\nabla^{k+2}v_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C(\|\nabla^{k+1}v_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|_{H^{k}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1)(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+1).

(4.16)

Combining
J_{2},J_{3}
with
J_{1}
and integrating in
t
, we find

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\frac{\alpha}{8}\|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\frac{\lambda}{4L}\|\nabla^{k+2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\|\nabla^{k+2}v_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\,dt+\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{1}{L}\|\nabla^{k+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle+C\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\left(\|\nabla^{k+1}v_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|_{H^{k}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\right)\,dt

\displaystyle+C\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\,dt+C.

(4.17)

To estimate the
L^{2}
-norm of
\nabla^{k+1}\partial_{t}Q_{L}
, we applying
\nabla^{k+1}
to (
2.36
) and multiplying by
\nabla^{k+1}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})
to have

\displaystyle J_{4}:=\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{k+1}\left(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}(\partial_{t}Q_{L}+v_{L}\cdot\nabla Q_{L})R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\right),\nabla^{k+1}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{k+1}\left(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}[Q_{L},\Omega_{L}])R_{Q_{L}}\right),\nabla^{k+1}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{k+1}\left(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})R_{Q_{L}}\right),\nabla^{k+1}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{k+1}\frac{1}{L}g_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L}),\nabla^{k+1}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})\right\rangle\,dxdt=:J_{5}+J_{6}.

(4.18)

Repeating the same argument in (
4.10
), we observe

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{k+1}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|\nabla^{k+1}\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+C\|\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{H^{k}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+1)

(4.19)

and

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{k+1}\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leq C\|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+C(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+1)^{2}.

(4.20)

Using (
2.2
), (
4.11
), (
4.19
)-(
4.20
) and (
4.3
) to
J_{5}
, we obtain

\displaystyle J_{5}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}(\nabla_{\nu}\partial_{p^{\nu}}f_{E}-\partial_{Q}f_{E}),\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{t}Q_{L}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+1}\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})|\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}||\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla R_{Q_{L}}||\nabla^{\mu_{3}}R_{Q_{L}}|\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+1}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})|\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\mathcal{H}||\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla R_{Q_{L}}||\nabla^{\mu_{3}}R_{Q_{L}}|\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\nu}_{ij}p^{\gamma}_{mn}}f_{E}\nabla^{k}\nabla^{3}_{\mu\gamma\nu}(Q_{L})_{mn}\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{t}(Q_{L})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+1}\partial_{t}Q_{L}|\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\nabla\partial^{2}_{pp}f_{E}||\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla^{2}Q_{L}|\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+1}\partial_{t}Q_{L}|\Big(|\nabla^{k+1}(\partial^{2}_{pQ}f_{E}\cdot\nabla Q_{L})|+|\nabla^{k+1}\partial_{Q}f_{E}|\Big)\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\eta\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+1}\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})|^{2}+|\nabla^{k+1}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{H^{k}(\mathbb{R}^{3})})\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\,dt+C

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\Big(\partial^{2}_{p_{ij}^{\nu}p_{mn}^{\gamma}}f_{E}\nabla^{k+1}\nabla_{\gamma}(Q_{L})_{mn}\nabla^{k+1}\nabla_{\nu}(Q_{L})_{ij}\Big)\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{2}\partial_{t}\partial^{2}_{p_{ij}^{\nu}p_{mn}^{\gamma}}f_{E}\nabla^{k+1}\nabla_{\gamma}(Q_{L})_{mn}\nabla^{k+1}\nabla_{\nu}(Q_{L})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{\nu}\partial^{2}_{p^{\nu}_{ij}p^{\gamma}_{mn}}f_{E}\nabla^{k}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\gamma}(Q_{L})_{mn}\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{t}(Q_{L})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\eta\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k+1}\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})|^{2}+|\nabla^{k+1}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}\partial_{t}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})|^{2}+|\nabla^{k+1}\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{H^{k}(\mathbb{R}^{3})})\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\,dt+C

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha}{4}\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\frac{\alpha}{8}\|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\frac{1}{8}\|\nabla^{k+1}\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\,dt

\displaystyle+C\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{H^{k}(\mathbb{R}^{3})})\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\,dt+C.

(4.21)

Utilizing Corollary

), (

\displaystyle J_{6}=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{L}\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla^{k+1}\partial_{t}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{ij}\nabla^{k}\left(\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}_{ij}\tilde{Q}_{kl}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})\nabla(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{kl}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2L}\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{t}\left(\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}_{ij}\tilde{Q}_{kl}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})\nabla^{k+1}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{kl}\nabla^{k+1}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{ij}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2L}\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{t}\left(\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}_{ij}\tilde{Q}_{kl}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})\right)\nabla^{k+1}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{kl}\nabla^{k+1}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{L}\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k}\partial_{t}\tilde{Q}_{L}|\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\nabla\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}\tilde{Q}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})||\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla\tilde{Q}_{L}|\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2L}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}_{ij}\tilde{Q}_{kl}}f_{B}\nabla^{k+1}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{kl}\nabla^{k+1}(\tilde{Q}_{L})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\eta\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{k}\partial_{t}(R^{T}_{Q_{L}}Q_{L}R_{Q_{L}})|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}\partial_{t}\tilde{Q}|^{2}+\frac{1}{L}|\nabla^{k+2}\tilde{Q}_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{L}|\nabla^{k}\tilde{Q}_{L}|^{2}|\partial_{t}Q_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{L^{2}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}\nabla\partial^{2}_{\tilde{Q}\tilde{Q}}f_{B}(\tilde{Q}_{L})|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla\tilde{Q}_{L}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\lambda}{4L}\|\nabla^{k+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+C\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\frac{1}{L^{2}}\|\nabla^{k+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{4}\,dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla^{k+1}[Q_{L},\mathcal{H}]_{ij}\nabla^{k+1}\nabla_{j}(v_{L})_{i}\,dxdt.

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{\mu_{1}+\mu_{2}+\mu_{3}=k}|\nabla^{\mu_{1}}(\mathcal{H}(Q_{L},\nabla Q_{L})+\frac{1}{L}g_{B}(Q_{L}))|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{2}}\nabla R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}|\nabla^{\mu_{3}}R_{Q_{L}}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+\eta_{1}(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|_{H^{k+1}}^{2})

\displaystyle\hskip-17.22217pt+\eta_{1}\int_{\tau_{L}}^{s}\|\nabla^{k+3}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|\nabla^{k+1}\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\frac{1}{L}\|\nabla^{k+2}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\,dt

\displaystyle\times\Big(\|\nabla^{k+2}Q_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|\nabla^{k+1}v_{L}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}Q_{L}\|_{H^{k}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{L}\|\nabla^{k+1}(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\Big)\,dt+C.

(4.34)

We apply the Gronwall inequality to (
4.34
) with (
4.2
) for
t\in(\tau_{L},s)
and conclude that (
4.1
) holds for
