on the
(\tau,s)
. Since
\tau\geq T_{0}
is an arbitrary positive constant, we prove (
4.1
) for any
s\in(\tau,T_{M}]
and
m=k+1
which completes a proof of this lemma.
∎

Proof of Theorem
3
.

Let
(Q,v)
be the strong solution to (
1.17
)-(
1.19
) in
\mathbb{R}^{3}\times[0,T^{\ast})
with initial data
(Q_{0},v_{0})\in H^{2}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\times H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, where
T^{\ast}
is its maximal existence time. Given any
T\in(0,T^{\ast})
, set

M=2\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(\nabla Q,v)\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}.

It follows from using Theorem
2
, that there exists a subsequence
(Q_{L},v_{L})
and

(\nabla Q_{L},v_{L})\rightarrow(\nabla Q,v),\qquad\text{in }~~L^{\infty}(0,T_{M};L_{loc}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))\cap L^{2}(0,T_{M};H_{loc}^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})).

Suppose that
T_{M}<T
. By Lemma
4.1
with
m=2
, note that

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{L}(x,T_{M})|^{2}\,dx\leq C.

Similarly to Lemma
2.4
, one can show the energy identity of the system (
1.11
) - (
1.13
)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(f_{E}(Q,\nabla Q)+\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}\right)(x,s)\,dx+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left|H(Q,\nabla Q)\right|^{2}\,dxdt+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla v|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big(f_{E}(Q_{0},\nabla Q_{0})+\frac{|v_{0}|^{2}}{2}\Big)\,dx.

(4.35)

Then by comparing (
4.35
) with (
2.29
) (c.f. Lemma 4.3
[
11
]
) and
integrating by parts, using Hölder’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\quad\lim\limits_{L\rightarrow 0}\|(\nabla^{2}Q_{L}-\nabla^{2}Q)(T_{M})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{L\rightarrow 0}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|(\nabla Q_{L}-\nabla Q)(T_{M})|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|(\nabla^{3}Q_{L}-\nabla^{3}Q)(T_{M})|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}=0.

Similarly, we find

\lim\limits_{L\rightarrow 0}\|(\nabla v_{L}-\nabla v)(T_{M})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=0,\quad\lim\limits_{L\rightarrow 0}\frac{1}{L}\||\nabla(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))|^{2})(T_{M})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}=0.

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\lim\limits_{L\rightarrow 0}\left(\|\nabla Q_{L}(T_{M})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|v_{L}(T_{M})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{1}{L}\|(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))(T_{M})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\nabla Q(T_{M})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|v(T_{M})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leq\frac{M}{2}.

Hence, for sufficiently small
L
, one has

\|\nabla Q_{L}(T_{M})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|v_{L}(T_{M})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\frac{1}{L}\|(Q_{L}-\pi(Q_{L}))(T_{M})\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\leq M.

Utilizing Proposition
3.1
with the initial data
(Q_{L}(T_{M}),v_{L}(T_{M}))
, we extend the strong solution
(Q_{L},v_{L})
to the time
T_{1}=:\min\{T,2T_{M}\}>T_{M}
. That is

(\nabla Q_{L},v_{L})\rightarrow(\nabla Q,v),\qquad\text{in }~~L^{\infty}(0,T_{1};L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))\cap L^{2}(0,T_{1};H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})).

(4.36)

Repeating the same argument with
T
, we establish the convergence up to
T
for any
T<T^{\ast}
and complete the first part of Theorem
3
as any sequence
L\to 0
due to the uniqueness of the solution
(Q,v)
. In the view of the Lemma
4.1
, the argument in the proof of (
1.20
) in Theorem
3
leads to the statement (
1.21
).
∎

5.
Appendix: Local existence and proof of Theorem
1

Assume the initial data
(Q_{L,0},v_{L,0})\in H^{2}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})\times H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
satisfies that

\|Q_{L,0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leq K
,
\div v_{L,0}=0
and

\displaystyle\|Q_{L,0}\|^{2}_{H^{2}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v_{L,0}\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}=M_{1}.

(5.1)

For any
f(x)\in H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})
, it follows from the Gagliardo–Nirenberg interpolation that

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f(x)|^{4}\,dx\leq\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f(x)|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla f(x)|^{2}\,dx\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f(x)|^{4}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\eta\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla f(x)|^{2}\,dx+\frac{C}{\eta^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f(x)|^{2}\,dx.

(5.2)

With the aid of (
5.2
), we now prove the local existence of (
1.11
)-(
1.13
) with initial data
(Q_{L,0},v_{L,0})
.

Proof of Theorem
1
.

Without loss of generality, we assume
L=1
and omit the subscript
L
in the proof. Define the space

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}(0,T)=\Big\{(Q,v):

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\big(\|Q(t)\|_{H^{2}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|v(t)\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\big)+\|\nabla^{3}Q\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}Q\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}v\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}\leq C_{1}M_{1},

\displaystyle\nabla\cdot v=0,\quad\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|Q(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leq 2K\Big\}

for some
T
and
C_{1}
to be chosen later.

For a given pair
(Q_{m},v_{m})\in\mathcal{V}(0,T)
, there exists a unique strong solution
(Q_{m+1},v_{m+1})
with the initial data
(Q_{0},v_{0})
of
the linearized system of (
1.11
)-(
1.13
):

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}-\Delta)v_{m+1}+\nabla P_{m+1}-\nabla\cdot[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]

\displaystyle=-v_{m}\cdot\nabla v_{m}-\nabla\cdot\sigma_{ij}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m}),

(5.3)

\displaystyle\nabla\cdot v_{m+1}=0,

(5.4)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}+[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]-h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})=-v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}+g_{B}(Q_{m})

(5.5)

for some
T_{m+1}>0
, where

\displaystyle h_{ij}(Q_{m},Q_{m+1}):=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\nabla_{\beta}[\partial_{p_{ij}^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})]+\nabla_{\beta}[\partial_{p_{ji}^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})]\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\left(\partial_{Q_{ij}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})-\partial_{Q_{ji}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{\delta_{ij}}{3}\sum_{l=1}^{3}\left(\nabla_{\beta}[\partial_{p_{ll}^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})]-\partial_{Q_{ll}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})\right).

(5.6)

Claim 1
: There exists a uniform
T_{M_{1}}
such that

(Q_{m+1},v_{m+1})\in\mathcal{V}(0,T_{M_{1}})
for some
T_{M_{1}}\leq T_{m+1}
for all
m\geq 1
.

To establish the
L^{2}
-norm of
\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}.
we multiply (
5.5
) with
\Delta^{2}Q_{m+1}
and observe

\displaystyle\left\langle\left(\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}+[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]-\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\right),\Delta^{2}Q_{m+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})-v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}+g_{B}(Q_{m}),\Delta^{2}Q_{m+1}\right\rangle.

(5.7)

We can compute the second term in the left-hand side of (
5.7
)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}],\Delta^{2}Q_{m+1}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{8}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{m}|^{2}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}|\nabla v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{8}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m+1}|^{2}\left(|\nabla Q_{m}|^{4}+|\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|^{2}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{8}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dx\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|^{2}\,dxdt+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m+1}|^{2}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.8)

Using the Sobolev inequality and (
5.1
), we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T_{m+1}}\left(\|\nabla Q_{m+1}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v_{m+1}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\right)\leq CM_{1}.

(5.9)

We employ the inequalities (
5.2
) and (
5.9
) to show the following:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m+1}|^{2}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|^{2}\,dxdt\leq\int_{0}^{s}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m+1}|^{4}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|^{4}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla v_{m+1}|^{2}+|v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dx\right)\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\eta_{1}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m}|^{3}+\frac{C}{\eta^{3}_{1}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|^{2}\,dx\right)dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}}).

(5.10)

Then we can write (
5.8
) as

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}],\Delta^{2}Q_{m+1}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{8}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}}).

(5.11)

Integrating by parts and using (
2.2
), we deduce the third term in (
5.7
) to

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}_{ij}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\Delta^{2}(Q_{m+1})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\nu}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla^{3}_{\mu\gamma\nu}(Q_{m+1})_{kl}\nabla^{3}_{\beta\gamma\mu}(Q_{m+1})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{\gamma}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p_{kl}^{\nu}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla^{2}_{\mu\nu}(Q_{m+1})_{kl}\nabla^{3}_{\beta\gamma\mu}(Q_{m+1})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{\gamma}\left(\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}Q_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla_{\mu}(Q_{m})_{kl}\right)\nabla^{3}_{\beta\gamma\mu}(Q_{m+1})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{3\alpha}{8}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dx+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{m+1}|^{2}(|\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{m}|^{4})\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{3\alpha}{8}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dx+CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}}),

(5.12)

where we used the argument of (
5.10
) in the last calculation.

Using the argument in (
5.10
) again, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla\left(\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})-v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}+g_{B}(Q_{m})\right)|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{m}|^{4}+|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|^{2}+|\nabla v_{m}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m}|^{2}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}\,dxdt\leq CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}})+CM_{1}s.

(5.13)

In view of (
5.11
)-(
5.13
), we deduce (
5.7
) to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\frac{\alpha}{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{0}|^{2}\,dx+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}})+CM_{1}s.

(5.14)

In order to estimate the
L^{2}
-norm of
\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}
, we multiply (
5.5
) by
\Delta\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}
and compute

\displaystyle\left\langle\left(\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}+[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]-\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\right),\Delta\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})-v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}+g_{B}(Q_{m}),\Delta\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}\right\rangle.

(5.15)

Using a similar argument to (
5.11
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}],\Delta\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+CK^{2}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}}).

(5.16)

Using (
2.2
), we compute the third term in (
5.15
)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\left(-\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\right),\Delta\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\gamma}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla^{2}_{\gamma\nu}(Q_{m+1})_{kl}\partial_{t}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\nu}(Q_{m+1})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}Q{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla_{\nu}(Q_{m+1})_{kl}\partial_{t}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\nu}(Q_{m+1})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{s}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\gamma}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla^{2}_{\gamma\nu}(Q_{m+1})_{kl}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\nu}(Q_{m+1})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{m+1}|\left(|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{m}||\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}|+|\partial_{t}Q_{m}||\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{m+1}|\left(|\nabla Q_{m}||\partial_{t}Q_{m}||\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}|\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}\nabla Q_{m+1}|\left(|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}||\nabla Q_{m}|+|\nabla Q_{m+1}||\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}\right)\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}\nabla Q_{m+1}||\nabla Q_{m+1}||\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha}{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\frac{\Lambda(1+4K^{2})}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{0}|^{2}\,dx+CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}})

\displaystyle+CM_{1}^{5}s+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\alpha}{8}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{8}|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt,

(5.17)

where in the last step, we used the argument in (
5.10
) and the following estimate

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{m+1}|^{2}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CM_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\eta|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}+\frac{1}{\eta^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt\leq\frac{\alpha}{16}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+CM_{1}^{5}s.

We can apply (
5.16
), (
5.17
) and (
5.13
) to give

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha}{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\frac{1}{2}\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\Lambda(1+4K^{2})}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{0}|^{2}\,dx+\frac{\alpha}{4}\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}})+CM_{1}^{5}s.

(5.18)

Adding (
5.18
) to (
5.14
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}+|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{0}|^{2}\,dx+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dx+CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}})+CM_{1}^{5}s+CM_{1}s.

(5.19)

Here
C
only depends on the following constants
\alpha,K
and
\Lambda
.

To estimate
\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}
in (
5.19
), we multiply (
5.3
) by
-\Delta v_{m+1}
and compute

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla v_{m+1}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]_{ij}\nabla_{j}\Delta(v_{m+1})_{i}\,dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla v_{0}|^{2}\,dx-\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\Big((v_{m})_{j}\nabla_{j}(v_{m})_{i}+\nabla_{j}\sigma_{ij}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})\Big)(\Delta v_{m+1})_{i}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla v_{0}|^{2}\,dx+\frac{1}{4}\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt+CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}}).

(5.20)

To cancel the term involving
h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})
in (
5.20
), we differentiate (
5.5
) in
x
, multiply by
\nabla h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})
and obtain

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla_{\beta}\left(\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}+[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]\right),\nabla_{\beta}h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})\right\rangle\,dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla_{\beta}\left(-v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}+g_{B}(Q_{m})\right),\nabla_{\beta}h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})\right\rangle\,dx.

(5.21)

we choose
A=Q,B=h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1}),F=\Delta\nabla v
in Lemma
2.3
and obtain

\displaystyle\left\langle[Q_{m},\Delta\Omega_{m+1}],h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})\right\rangle=\left\langle\Delta\nabla v_{m+1},[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]\right\rangle.

(5.22)

Note that

h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})\leq C(|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}|+|\nabla Q_{m+1}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{m+1}||\nabla Q_{m}|).

Then using (
5.22
), we compute the second term in (
5.21
)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla_{\beta}[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}],\nabla_{\beta}h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle[Q_{m},\Delta\Omega_{m+1}],h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle[\Delta Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]+2[\nabla Q_{m},\nabla\Omega_{m+1}],h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle[Q_{m},\Delta\Omega_{m+1}],h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})\right\rangle\,dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle[\nabla Q_{m},\nabla\Omega_{m+1}],h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle[\nabla_{\alpha}Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}],\nabla_{\alpha}h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\Delta\nabla v_{m+1},[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]\right\rangle\,dx-\frac{1}{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})|^{2}\,dx-\eta_{1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m}|^{2}\,dx-CM_{1}^{2}\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}}.

(5.23)

We repeat the argument in (
5.17
) for the first term in (
5.21
), apply Young’s inequality to the right-hand side of (
5.21
). Then integrate (
5.21
) in
t
and combine with (
5.20
) yield

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla v_{m+1}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\frac{1}{2}\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla v_{0}|^{2}\,dx+\frac{\Lambda(1+4K^{2})}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{0}|^{2}\,dx+CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}})

\displaystyle+\eta_{2}\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}+|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta_{2})M_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}})+C(\eta_{2})M_{1}^{5}s

(5.24)

for some small
\eta_{1},\eta_{2}
. Substituting (
5.19
) into (
5.24
) and choosing
\eta_{2}
sufficiently small, we obtain the estimates for
v_{m+1}
. Combining the resulting expression with (
5.19
) yields

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}(x,s)|^{2}+|\nabla v_{m+1}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{3}Q_{m+1}|^{2}+|\nabla\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CM_{1}+CM_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}})+CM_{1}^{5}s+CM_{1}s.

(5.25)

Here
C
only depends on
\alpha,K
and
\Lambda
.

It remains to check the
L^{2}
-norm of the lower order terms in
\mathcal{V}(0,T)
. We multiply (
5.3
) by
v_{m+1}
to obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m+1}(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\frac{1}{2}\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla v_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{0}|^{2}\,dx+C\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]|^{2}+|\sigma_{ij}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CM_{1}+C\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{m+1}|^{4}+|\nabla Q_{m}|^{4}\,dxdt\leq CM_{1}+CM_{1}s.

(5.26)

By using mean value theorem with the fact that
g_{B}(Q_{e})=0
, we find

\displaystyle|g_{B}(Q_{m})|\leq C(K)|Q_{m}-Q_{e}|.

(5.27)

Multiplying (
5.5
) by
\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}
and
Q_{m+1}-Q_{e}
respectively then using (
5.27
) yield

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|(Q_{m+1}-Q_{e})(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\frac{1}{2}\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{0}-Q_{e}|^{2}\,dx+\frac{1}{2}\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{m+1}-Q_{e}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int^{s}_{0}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]|^{2}+|h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})|^{2}+|v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}+|g_{B}(Q_{m})|^{2}dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CM_{1}+CM_{1}s.

(5.28)

Note that the
L^{2}
-norm of
\nabla Q_{m+1}
from the Sobolev inequality and (
5.28
). Now adding (
5.26
) and (
5.28
) to (
5.25
), we have

\displaystyle\big(\|Q(x,s)\|_{H^{2}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}+\|v(x,s)\|_{H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\big)+\|\nabla^{3}Q\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{t}Q\|_{L^{2}(0,T;H^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}+\|\nabla^{2}v\|_{L^{2}(0,T_{0};L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{1}}{4}M_{1}+\frac{C_{1}}{4}M_{1}^{2}(\eta_{1}+s+\frac{s}{\eta_{1}^{3}})+\frac{C_{1}}{4}M_{1}^{5}s+\frac{C_{1}}{4}M_{1}s\leq C_{1}M_{1}

(5.29)

for some
C_{1}
depending on
\alpha,K
and
\Lambda
. Here in the last step, we set
\eta_{1}=M_{1}^{-1}
and
s\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{2}M_{1}^{-4},\frac{1}{2}M_{1}^{-1},\frac{1}{2}\right\}
.

It remains to verify that
\|Q_{m+1}(x,s)\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leq 2K
. Note from (
5.28
) that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{m+1}(x,s)-Q_{0}|^{2}\,dx=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\int_{0}^{s}\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}\,dt\right)^{2}\,dx\leq s\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dt\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle s\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\int_{0}^{s}|\partial_{t}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dxdt\leq CM_{1}s(1+s)\leq CM_{1}s

for
s\leq\frac{1}{2}
. By using the Gagliardo–Nirenberg interpolation (c.f.
[
11
]
) and choosing
s\leq C_{2}^{-8}K^{8}M_{1}^{-4}
, we have

\displaystyle\|Q_{m+1}(s)-Q_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\leq

\displaystyle C\|Q_{m+1}(s)-Q_{0}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}(s)-Q_{0})\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{\frac{3}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{2}(M_{1}s)^{\frac{1}{8}}M_{1}^{\frac{3}{8}}\leq K,

where
C_{2}
is independent from
m
. Therefore, we prove Claim 1 by choosing

T_{M_{1}}:=\min\left\{C_{2}^{-8}K^{8}M_{1}^{-4},\frac{1}{2}M_{1}^{-4},\frac{1}{2}M_{1}^{-1},\frac{1}{2}\right\}.

Claim 2
: There exists
T>0
such that

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\big(\|Q_{m+1}-Q_{m}(t)\|_{H^{1}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v_{m+1}-v_{m}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)

\displaystyle+\|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}+\|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\big(\|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}(t)\|_{H^{1}_{Q_{e}}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}+\|v_{m}-v_{m-1}(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}\big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}+\frac{1}{2}\|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{3}))}

For given pairs
(Q_{m},v_{m})
and
(Q_{m-1},v_{m-1})\in\mathcal{V}
, we have

\displaystyle(\partial_{t}-\Delta)(v_{m+1}-v_{m})+\nabla(P_{m+1}-P_{m})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla\cdot[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]-\nabla\cdot[Q_{m-1},h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})]

\displaystyle-v_{m}\cdot\nabla v_{m}+v_{{m-1}}\cdot\nabla v_{m-1}+\sigma(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m}))-\sigma(Q_{{m-1}},\nabla Q_{{m-1}})),

(5.30)

\displaystyle\nabla\cdot(v_{m+1}-v_{m})=0,

(5.31)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})+[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]-[Q_{m-1},\Omega_{m}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})-\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m})-v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}+v_{m-1}\cdot\nabla Q_{m-1}

\displaystyle-\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})+\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m-1})+g_{B}(Q_{m})-g_{B}(Q_{m-1}).

(5.32)

Multiplying (
5.32
) by
-\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})
yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})(x,s)|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]-[Q_{m-1},\Omega_{m}],\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})-\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m}),\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle-v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}+v_{m-1}\cdot\nabla Q_{m-1},\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle-\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})+\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m-1}),\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle g_{B}(Q_{m})-g_{B}(Q_{m-1}),\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt.

(5.33)

Using Young’s inequality and (
5.2
), we compute the first term in the right-hand side of (
5.33
)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]-[Q_{m-1},\Omega_{m}],\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt+C(\eta)\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{0}^{s}\|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt+C(\eta)\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta)M_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{4}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt+CM_{1}^{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt,

(5.34)

where
\eta
and
\eta_{1}
are some small constants to be chosen later.

Applying (
5.2
) again to the second term in (
5.33
) and using (
2.2
) yields

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})-\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m}),\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\nu}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla^{2}_{\gamma\nu}(Q_{m+1})_{kl}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\gamma}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\nu}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m})\nabla^{2}_{\gamma\nu}(Q_{m})_{kl}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\gamma}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}Q_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla_{\gamma}(Q_{m})_{kl}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\gamma}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}Q_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m})\nabla_{\gamma}(Q_{m-1})_{kl}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\gamma}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-(\frac{\alpha}{2}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\nu}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla^{2}_{\gamma\nu}(Q_{m})_{kl}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\gamma}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\nu}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m})\nabla^{2}_{\gamma\nu}(Q_{m})_{kl}\nabla^{2}_{\beta\gamma}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial^{2}_{pQ}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla Q_{m}-\partial^{2}_{pQ}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m})\nabla Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-(\frac{\alpha}{2}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{0}^{s}\left(\|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{3})}^{2}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}Q_{m}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(|\nabla Q_{m+1}-\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2})|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta)\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{m}-\nabla Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-(\frac{\alpha}{2}-2\eta)\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt+\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(M_{1}+M_{1}^{4})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.35)

The remaining terms in (
5.33
) are

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle-v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}+v_{m-1}\cdot\nabla Q_{m-1},\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle-\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})+\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m-1}),\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle g_{B}(Q_{m})-g_{B}(Q_{m-1}),\Delta(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{m}-\nabla Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{m-1}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{m}|^{4}|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\nabla Q_{m-1}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}\right)|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(M_{1}+M_{1}^{4})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.36)

Substituting (
5.34
)-(
5.36
) into (
5.33
), we find

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\frac{\alpha}{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(M_{1}+M_{1}^{4})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.37)

Now, we compute the difference
Q_{m+1}-Q_{m}
. Multiplying
(
5.32
)
by
Q_{m+1}-Q_{m}
, one can show

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})(x,s)|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle CM_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{m+1}-Q_{m}|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(M_{1}+M_{1}^{4})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}+|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.38)

Combining (
5.38
) with (
5.37
), we find

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})(x,s)|^{2}+|\nabla(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})(x,s)|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|Q_{m+1}-Q_{m}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(M_{1}+M_{1}^{4})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}+|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.39)

Next we compute the difference involving
v_{m}
. Multiplying (
5.30
) by
(v_{m+1}-v_{m})
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|(v_{m+1}-v_{m})(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\frac{3}{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\sigma(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m}))-\sigma(Q_{{m-1}},\nabla Q_{{m-1}})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla\cdot[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]-\nabla\cdot[Q_{m-1},h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})],v_{m+1}-v_{m}\right\rangle\,dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle-v_{m}\cdot\nabla v_{m}+v_{{m-1}}\cdot\nabla v_{m-1},v_{m+1}-v_{m}\right\rangle\,dxdt.

(5.40)

Using (
5.2
), we find

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\sigma(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})-\sigma(Q_{{m-1}},\nabla Q_{{m-1}})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}\left(|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{m-1}|^{2}\right)+|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{m-1}|^{4}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}dxdt+C(M_{1}+M_{1}^{4})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}dxdt.

(5.41)

Applying (
5.2
) to the last term in (
5.40
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle-v_{m}\cdot\nabla v_{m}+v_{{m-1}}\cdot\nabla v_{m-1},v_{m+1}-v_{m}\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(-v_{m}\cdot\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})+(v_{m-1}-v_{m})\cdot\nabla v_{m-1}\right)_{j}(v_{m+1}-v_{m})_{j}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m}||\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})||v_{m+1}-v_{m}|\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m-1}|(|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})||v_{m+1}-v_{m}|+|v_{m}-v_{m-1}||\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|)\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+CM_{1}^{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m+1}-v_{m}|^{2}+|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.42)

Thus we can write (
5.40
) as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|(v_{m+1}-v_{m})(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+CM_{1}^{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|v_{m+1}-v_{m}|^{2}+|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla\cdot[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]-\nabla\cdot[Q_{m-1},h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})],(v_{m+1}-v_{m})\right\rangle\,dx.

(5.43)

To cancel the Lie bracket term in (
5.43
), it follows from Lemma
2.3
with the substitution

A=Q_{m},B=h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1}),F=\Omega_{m+1}
and the other three cases that

\displaystyle\left\langle[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]-[Q_{m-1},\Omega_{m}],h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})-h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m}))\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]-[Q_{m-1},h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})],\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})\right\rangle.

(5.44)

Multiplying (
5.32
) by

h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})-h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})
and using (
5.44
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla\cdot[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]-\nabla\cdot[Q_{m-1},h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})],(v_{m+1}-v_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})-h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m}),h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})-h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle-v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}+v_{m-1}\cdot\nabla Q_{m-1},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})-h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle g_{B}(Q_{m})-g_{B}(Q_{m-1}),h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})-h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})-h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})|^{2}\,dxdt+\eta_{3}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta_{3})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m})-\partial_{Q}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta_{3})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}-v_{m-1}\cdot\nabla Q_{m-1}|^{2}+|g_{B}(Q_{m})-g_{B}(Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m}),\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})-\nabla_{\beta}\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1}\right\rangle\,dxdt.

(5.45)

In a similar calculation to (
5.35
), using (
2.2
), we estimate the last term in (
5.45
)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m}),\nabla_{\beta}\left(\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})-\partial_{p^{\beta}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m}\right)\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\nu}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla^{2}_{\beta\nu}(Q_{m+1})_{kl}\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\nu}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m})\nabla^{2}_{\beta\nu}(Q_{m})_{kl}\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}Q_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla_{\beta}(Q_{m})_{kl}\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}Q_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m})\nabla_{\beta}(Q_{m-1})_{kl}\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\nu}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla^{2}_{\beta\nu}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{kl}\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\nu}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla^{2}_{\beta\nu}(Q_{m})_{kl}\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial^{2}_{p^{\beta}_{ij}p^{\nu}_{kl}}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m})\nabla^{2}_{\beta\nu}(Q_{m})_{kl}\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})_{ij}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\eta_{3}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(\eta_{3})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial^{2}_{pQ}f_{E}(Q_{m},\nabla Q_{m+1})\nabla Q_{m}-\partial^{2}_{pQ}f_{E}(Q_{m-1},\nabla Q_{m})\nabla Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha}{4}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})(x,s)|^{2}\,dx+2\eta_{3}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\eta_{2}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt+CM_{1}^{2}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt+CM_{1}^{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.46)

Here we used the fact from (
5.5
) that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}Q_{m}|^{2}\,dx\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla v_{m+1}|^{2}+|v_{m}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}Q_{m+1}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle+C\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla Q_{m+1}|^{2}|\nabla Q_{m}|^{2}+|\nabla Q_{m}|^{4}+|g_{B}(Q_{m})|^{2}\,dx\leq CM_{1}.

For the term
\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})
, it follows from (
5.32
) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m}\cdot\nabla Q_{m}-v_{m-1}\cdot\nabla Q_{m-1}|^{2}+|[Q_{m},\Omega_{m+1}]-[Q_{m-1},\Omega_{m}]|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})-h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})|^{2}+|g_{B}(Q_{m})-g_{B}(Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+CM_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}+|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+CM_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}+|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.47)

Substituting (
5.46
) into (
5.45
) and using with sufficiently small
\eta_{3}
, we find

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left\langle\nabla\cdot[Q_{m},h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})]-\nabla\cdot[Q_{m-1},h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})],(v_{m+1}-v_{m})\right\rangle\,dxdt

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|h(Q_{m-1},Q_{m})-h(Q_{m},Q_{m+1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\eta_{2}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(M_{1}+M_{1}^{2}+M_{1}^{4})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}+|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(M_{1}+M_{1}^{4})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}+|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.48)

Adding (
5.48
) to (
5.43
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m+1}-v_{m}|^{2}(x,s)\,dx+\frac{1}{4}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\eta_{2}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(M_{1}+M_{1}^{2}+M_{1}^{4})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}+|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C(M_{1}+M_{1}^{4})\int_{0}^{s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}+|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}\,dxdt.

(5.49)

Substituting (
5.39
) into (
5.49
) and choosing suitable
\eta_{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|Q_{m+1}-Q_{m}|^{2}+|\nabla(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}+|v_{m+1}-v_{m}|^{2}\right)(x,s)\,dx

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m+1}-v_{m})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{3}\eta_{1}\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}+|\nabla^{2}(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}+|\nabla(v_{m}-v_{m-1})|^{2}\,dxdt

\displaystyle+C_{3}(M_{1}+M_{1}^{2}+M_{1}^{4})s\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|\nabla(Q_{m+1}-Q_{m})|^{2}+|\nabla(Q_{m}-Q_{m-1})|^{2}\right)(x,s)\,dx

\displaystyle+C_{3}(M_{1}+M_{1}^{4})s\sup_{0\leq s\leq T}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(|v_{m}-v_{m-1}|^{2}+|Q_{m}-Q_{m-1}|^{2}\right)(x,s)\,dx,

(5.50)

where
C_{3}
is a constant independent from
m
. Then for
m>1
, choosing
\eta_{1}=\frac{1}{8}C_{3}^{-1}
, we prove the claim 2 with

T:=\frac{1}{8C_{3}}\min\{M_{1}^{-1},M_{1}^{-2},M_{1}^{-4}\}.

Using the Claim 1,
(Q_{m+1},v_{m+1})
and
(Q_{m},v_{m})
have two limits. By Claim 2,
(Q_{m+1},v_{m+1})
is a Cauchy sequence in

L^{\infty}([0,T];H^{1}_{Q_{e}}\times L^{2})\cap L^{2}([0,T];H^{2}_{Q_{e}}\times H^{1}])
, so
two weak limit of
(Q_{m+1},v_{m+1})
and
(Q_{m},v_{m})
are the same. One can estimate
P_{m}
using (
5.3
) and the argument in (
3.29
)-(
3.30
). As
m\to\infty
, we prove Theorem
1
.
∎
